# CHANGELOG - ptydeck

Completed and validated release history belongs here.

## 2026-05-13

- [x] The frontend session-control layer now fails closed while the WebSocket/runtime connection is not `connected` instead of continuing to present write-capable composer, resize, and takeover states during a reconnect window that cannot satisfy backend attachment requirements yet. `frontend/src/public/session-control-runtime-state.js` now blocks implicit-owner fallback, write access, take/release/transfer/manage affordances, and session-control badge promotion while the connection state is still `connecting`, `reconnecting`, or another non-connected status, and it surfaces explicit reconnect-facing write-block and summary messages instead of allowing doomed write attempts to fall through to repeated backend `active attached session client` errors.
- [x] `frontend/src/public/session-control-runtime-controller.js` and `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-session-access-assembly.js` now thread the live store `connectionState` into that session-control gating context, so the fail-closed reconnect contract applies to the real command composer, quick-send, and terminal-control UI paths instead of existing only as a detached helper-layer rule.
- [x] Focused deterministic regressions for that reconnect gating now live in `frontend/test/session-control-runtime-state.test.js`, `frontend/test/session-control-runtime-controller.test.js`, and `frontend/test/app-runtime-session-access-assembly.test.js`. Validation passed with `node --check frontend/src/public/session-control-runtime-state.js frontend/src/public/session-control-runtime-controller.js frontend/src/public/app-runtime-session-access-assembly.js frontend/test/session-control-runtime-state.test.js frontend/test/session-control-runtime-controller.test.js`, `node --test frontend/test/session-control-runtime-state.test.js frontend/test/session-control-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/app-runtime-session-access-assembly.test.js`, `node --test --experimental-test-coverage frontend/test/session-control-runtime-state.test.js frontend/test/session-control-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/app-runtime-session-access-assembly.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] New-session creation now targets the active deck directly instead of relying on a second immediate `move-to-deck` request. `frontend/src/public/app-lifecycle-controller.js` now passes `deckId` into `POST /api/v1/sessions`, so fast-exiting sessions no longer trigger a false `Failed to create session.` path just because they disappeared before a follow-up move finished.
- [x] The backend request validator now recognizes that create-time `deckId` contract explicitly. `backend/src/validation.js` now validates `deckId` as a create-only string field for `POST /api/v1/sessions`, and `backend/test/validation.test.js` locks down both the accepted string path and the rejected non-string path.
- [x] Stale or ghost session cards are now removable again even when the live backend session record is already gone. `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-interactions-controller.js` now treats a missing live session record or a backend `404 SessionNotFound` delete response as a bounded stale-card cleanup path and removes the card locally instead of surfacing a permanent `Failed to delete session.` dead end.
- [x] Focused deterministic regressions for that lifecycle stabilization now live in `frontend/test/app-lifecycle-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js`, and `backend/test/validation.test.js`, covering active-deck create payloads, stale-card cleanup, backend `SessionNotFound` delete fallbacks, and the explicit validator contract for create-time `deckId`.
- [x] Persisted stopped-session restore now actually preserves the stopped lifecycle state instead of silently relaunching the PTY. `backend/src/session-manager-runtime-facade.js` now forwards `initialState` through `manager.create(...)`, which is the path used by `backend/src/runtime-startup-restore.js` when recreating intentionally stopped sessions from persisted state.
- [x] Direct deterministic regressions now lock down that restore contract at both levels: `backend/test/session-manager-runtime-facade.test.js` proves the facade forwards `initialState: "stopped"` instead of dropping it, and `backend/test/session-manager.test.js` proves a real `SessionManager` created with that initial state stays stopped and does not spawn a PTY.
- [x] The operator composer placement bootstrap no longer treats the pre-attach `409 OperatorClientRequired` response as a fatal initialization error. `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now recognizes that early startup race, suppresses the global UI error for that bounded case, and clears its one-shot initialization latch so a later retry or runtime snapshot can still hydrate placement state after the WebSocket/session-control attachment finishes.
- [x] Direct deterministic coverage for that composer-placement startup race now lives in `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js`, proving an early `OperatorClientRequired` response does not poison later placement initialization once the operator client is attached.
- [x] Exited session cards now use the same header `Play` affordance as stopped sessions instead of behaving like permanently dead cards. `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-render-controller.js` and `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-factory-controller.js` now render `state: exited` cards with an enabled `Start session` action, while `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-interactions-controller.js` routes that click through `restartSession(...)` instead of `startSession(...)` so the existing backend restart contract relaunches the PTY while preserving session identity.
- [x] The exited-session operator messaging now matches that behavior. `frontend/src/public/session-view-model.js` now tells operators that exited sessions can be relaunched from the header instead of claiming restart is disabled, and focused deterministic regressions now lock down the render, interaction, and messaging contract in `frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/session-card-factory-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js`, and `frontend/test/session-view-model.test.js`.
- [x] Validation for the exited-session header restart fix passed with `node --check frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-render-controller.js frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-factory-controller.js frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-interactions-controller.js frontend/src/public/session-view-model.js`, `node --test frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-factory-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-view-model.test.js`, `node --test --experimental-test-coverage frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-factory-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-view-model.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] Trusted-local session control now keeps a stable WebSocket-side client identity across frontend-only refreshes without adding a new local no-auth fetch failure path. `frontend/src/public/ws-runtime-controller.js` now uses `/auth/ws-ticket` only when a real bearer-authenticated WebSocket bootstrap is needed, while `AUTH_MODE=off` carries the trusted-local client id/label directly in a `ptydeck.client.<base64url-json>` subprotocol instead of issuing an extra `POST /auth/ws-ticket`.
- [x] `backend/src/runtime-access-policy.js` and `backend/src/runtime-ws-upgrade.js` now parse and consume that trusted-local WebSocket metadata in auth-disabled mode, while `frontend/src/public/app-bootstrap-composition-controller.js` explicitly forwards the trusted-local client payload into the WS runtime so session-control attachment state survives a frontend-only refresh without drifting back to an ephemeral connection id. Auth-disabled ws tickets remain accepted only as a bounded compatibility path for older clients.
- [x] Direct deterministic regression coverage for that trusted-local refresh/control transport fix now lives in `frontend/test/ws-runtime-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/app-bootstrap-composition-controller.test.js`, `backend/test/runtime-access-policy.test.js`, and `backend/test/runtime-ws-upgrade.test.js`. Validation passed with `node --check frontend/src/public/ws-runtime-controller.js frontend/src/public/app-bootstrap-composition-controller.js backend/src/runtime-access-policy.js backend/src/runtime-ws-upgrade.js backend/src/runtime.js`, `node --test frontend/test/ws-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/app-bootstrap-composition-controller.test.js backend/test/runtime-access-policy.test.js backend/test/runtime-ws-upgrade.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] The frontend WebSocket client now ignores stale `open`, `message`, `error`, and `close` events from earlier socket generations after a reconnect attempt has already advanced the connection lifecycle. `frontend/src/public/ws-client.js` now binds each listener to the active connect generation and socket instance, so a late event from an older socket can no longer knock the UI back into `reconnecting` or schedule an extra reconnect after a newer connection has already taken over.
- [x] Direct deterministic regression coverage for that reconnect-state fix now lives in `frontend/test/ws-client.test.js`, including an explicit late-close/late-error scenario from an earlier socket after a successful reconnect, while `frontend/test/ws-runtime-controller.test.js` remains green against the same runtime contract.
- [x] Validation for the WebSocket reconnect-state fix passed with `node --test frontend/test/ws-client.test.js frontend/test/ws-runtime-controller.test.js`, `node --test --experimental-test-coverage frontend/test/ws-client.test.js frontend/test/ws-runtime-controller.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] Stopped sessions are now also visually identifiable in the deck sidebar. `frontend/src/public/ui/deck-sidebar-controller.js` now marks stopped session entries with a dedicated `stopped` class, and `frontend/src/public/styles.css` now dims the sidebar quick id plus session name to `50%` opacity for those entries while leaving the row layout, click target, and activity indicator placement unchanged.
- [x] Direct deterministic coverage for that sidebar stopped-state contract now lives in `frontend/test/deck-sidebar-controller.test.js` and `frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js`, proving the FE render path marks stopped entries and the CSS contract dims only the intended text tokens.
- [x] Validation for the sidebar stopped-session indicator passed with `node --test frontend/test/deck-sidebar-controller.test.js frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] The session-header lifecycle toggle now ships real `Play` / `Stop` icon masks instead of only toggling class names that had no CSS mask backing. `frontend/src/public/styles.css` now defines `--icon-tabler-player-play-filled-mask` and `--icon-tabler-player-stop-filled-mask` plus the corresponding `.icon-tabler-player-play-filled` and `.icon-tabler-player-stop-filled` mappings, so stopped sessions can render a visible play affordance and running sessions can render a visible stop affordance.
- [x] Styles contract coverage now locks down those lifecycle icon mask mappings in `frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js`, so a future header toggle refactor cannot silently regress to invisible icon classes again.
- [x] Validation for the lifecycle icon mask fix passed with `node --test frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js frontend/test/session-card-factory-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] The session-header lifecycle toggle now renders the correct icon immediately on first card paint instead of inheriting the template default until a later runtime rerender. `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-factory-controller.js` now applies the same start-versus-stop icon, `aria-label`, and tooltip contract during initial card creation that `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-render-controller.js` already enforces on later updates, so restored or newly rendered stopped sessions show the `Play` affordance from the start.
- [x] The session-header lifecycle toggle now shares the same color and icon sizing contract as the neighboring `Refresh` and `Settings` controls. `frontend/src/public/styles.css` now styles `.session-start-stop` and `.session-start-stop-icon` in the same grouped rules, keeping the header action strip visually consistent.
- [x] Validation for the session-header lifecycle icon follow-up passed with `node --test frontend/test/session-card-factory-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] The session-header `Start` / `Stop` toggle is now actually wired through the live frontend session-card path instead of rendering as a dead control. `frontend/src/public/ui/session-grid-controller.js` now forwards `startStopBtn` into the session-card interaction binding and both `startStopBtn` plus `startStopIconEl` into the mounted terminal-card refs, while `frontend/src/public/ui/session-terminal-runtime-controller.js` now persists those refs on the live terminal entry so the toolbar click handler and later render updates operate on the real mounted card.
- [x] Direct deterministic regressions now lock down that lifecycle-toggle wiring contract in `frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js` and `frontend/test/session-terminal-runtime-controller.test.js`, proving the grid/controller seam forwards the header refs and the mounted terminal entry retains them for later start/stop state updates.
- [x] Validation for the session-header start/stop wiring fix passed with `node --test frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-terminal-runtime-controller.test.js`, `node --test --experimental-test-coverage frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-terminal-runtime-controller.test.js`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] The composer action column no longer overshoots the shared input height. `frontend/src/public/styles.css` now makes the stacked `Normalize`, `Repair`, and `Send` buttons stretch inside one grid column that inherits the shared composer min-height, so the footer and overlay action stack stays aligned with the input box instead of rendering taller than the textarea baseline.
- [x] The persisted session start/stop lifecycle now respects the existing session-control ownership model instead of bypassing it. `POST /api/v1/sessions/{sessionId}/start` and `POST /api/v1/sessions/{sessionId}/stop` now enforce the same controller-access contract as direct terminal input and resize, and the session-card toolbar now routes blocked start/stop clicks into the normal reclaim/error path instead of silently allowing cross-client lifecycle mutation.
- [x] Stopped cards now stay visually empty even when the composer placement mode is `active-overlay` or a per-terminal overlay was pinned earlier. The overlay composer host is now suppressed for stopped sessions until they are started again, while pinned overlay draft state remains preserved server-side for later reuse after restart.
- [x] Restored stopped secret-backed SSH sessions now expose an explicit API/UI start-block status instead of a misleading generic `Start` affordance. The backend now publishes `startBlockedReason: remote-secret-unavailable` when a stopped SSH session lost its non-persisted runtime secret during restore, the session-view-model surfaces a concrete operator message, and the session toolbar disables `Start` for that fail-closed case.
- [x] Validation for the start/stop quality follow-up passed with `node --test backend/test/runtime-session-dispatch.test.js backend/test/session-manager-session-runtime.test.js`, `node --test frontend/test/session-view-model.test.js frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js`, `npm run docs:generate`, `npm run lint`, `npm run docs:check`, and `git diff --check`.
- [x] Session cards now expose a persisted start/stop lifecycle toggle directly in the terminal header beside `Refresh` and `Settings`. Running sessions render a stop affordance, stopped sessions render a start affordance, and the backend now persists the resulting `stopped` versus `running` state across restart through new `POST /api/v1/sessions/{sessionId}/start` and `POST /api/v1/sessions/{sessionId}/stop` routes plus startup restore handling for intentionally stopped sessions.
- [x] Stopping a session now preserves the visible card and reserved layout slot while clearing the terminal viewport itself, so the session stays in the deck/grid but its terminal area remains visually empty until restarted. Frontend session interaction, resize, quick-send, and direct-typing paths now explicitly fail closed for stopped sessions instead of treating them like active or exited terminals.
- [x] Focused lifecycle coverage for the shipped start/stop contract now lives across `backend/test/runtime-session-dispatch.test.js`, `backend/test/runtime-startup-restore.test.js`, `backend/test/session-manager-session-runtime.test.js`, `backend/test/session-manager-launch-runtime.test.js`, `backend/test/runtime-status-reporting.test.js`, and the related frontend session-card/runtime/controller tests. One backend restore-create seam regression in `backend/test/runtime-session-resource-authority.test.js` was closed by making `initialState` forwarding explicit only when set, so the restored-session manager payload remains deterministic for both normal and intentionally stopped restore paths.
- [x] Closeout validation for the persisted session start/stop feature passed with `npm run docs:generate`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, `node --test backend/test/runtime-session-dispatch.test.js backend/test/runtime-startup-restore.test.js backend/test/session-manager-session-runtime.test.js backend/test/session-manager-runtime-facade.test.js backend/test/session-manager-launch-runtime.test.js backend/test/runtime-status-reporting.test.js backend/test/contract-conformance.test.js`, `node --test frontend/test/api-client.test.js frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js frontend/test/runtime-event-controller.test.js frontend/test/command-composer-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-quick-send-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/command-executor-custom-handlers.test.js frontend/test/session-terminal-resize-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-runtime-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-view-model.test.js frontend/test/template-contract.test.js frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js frontend/test/contract-alignment.test.js`, `npm --prefix backend run test:coverage`, the focused frontend coverage lane for those changed tests, and `git diff --check`. `npm run test` and `npm run test:coverage:check` were both retried and then terminated after the known frontend open-handle stall in the broad `frontend` lane, per repo hygiene rules.
- [x] `CMP-406`, `CMP-407`, and `CMP-408` are now complete as the first delivered slices inside `v0.4.0-H184`. The shared footer composer and pinned overlay composers now expose an explicit opt-in `Repair` action beside `Normalize`, and `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now drives a preview/apply shell that keeps the original draft recoverable until the operator explicitly applies the repaired candidate.
- [x] The first shipped structure-aware repair engines now live in `frontend/src/public/composer-repair-runtime.js`. The shell-family repair slice now supports bounded Bash/POSIX shell, PowerShell, and CMD reconstruction for strong hard-wrap cases such as quoted-argument joins, path/URL continuation, explicit line-continuation collapse, and wrapped argument-value joins, while the JSON slice now repairs wrapped JSON string values only when strict `JSON.parse` validation succeeds. Ambiguous or weak-confidence input still fails closed and leaves the draft unchanged.
- [x] Direct deterministic regression coverage for the new repair flow now lives in `frontend/test/composer-repair-runtime.test.js` and `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js`, while the shared template and app integration contracts were updated in `frontend/test/template-contract.test.js` and `frontend/test/app.test.js`. The operator docs in `README.md`, `docs/manual/paste-and-send-safety.md`, and `CODEX_CONTEXT.md` now describe the shipped `Repair` preview/apply contract and the currently supported shell-family plus JSON repair families.
- [x] `CMP-409` and `CMP-410` are now complete, fully closing `v0.4.0-H184`. `frontend/src/public/composer-repair-runtime.js` now adds conservative XML repair for wrapped text and attribute-value continuation behind strict browser parser validation, and the deterministic repair regressions now cover XML corpora plus explicit preview-cancel original-draft preservation.
- [x] The shared footer composer and pinned overlay composers now expose an explicit `Normalize` action above `Send` for whitespace cleanup before submission. `frontend/src/public/index.html`, `frontend/src/public/styles.css`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-dom-refs.js`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-composition-controller.js`, and `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now wire one shared-footer normalize button plus pinned-overlay normalize buttons through the same server-persisted composer-placement draft authority.
- [x] The shipped normalize behavior is intentionally conservative: it normalizes CRLF to LF, strips trailing horizontal whitespace on each line, and trims outer whitespace without rewriting internal indentation structure. Focused regression coverage for the shared-footer and pinned-overlay normalize flows now lives in `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js`, with template coverage for the new shared normalize control in `frontend/test/template-contract.test.js`.
- [x] The normalize action now also trims leading horizontal whitespace on every line instead of only trimming the outer text boundary. Shared-footer and pinned-overlay regression coverage in `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js` now locks down the line-wise cleanup contract for multiline input.
- [x] The shared footer composer and pinned overlay composers now preserve the active browser undo stack by refusing to programmatically rewrite a focused textarea from server-side placement state. `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now keeps focused shared and pinned composer inputs locally authoritative for DOM value while the operator is actively editing, and `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js` now locks down that focused shared-footer no-overwrite contract.
- [x] The lightweight backend runtime is now the default local dev path instead of the opt-in variant. Root `package.json` and `backend/package.json` now make `npm run dev` and `npm --prefix backend run dev` skip `local-config/ptydeck/backend.env.local` by default, while `npm run dev:env`, `npm --prefix backend run dev:env`, or `PTYDECK_BACKEND_LOAD_LOCAL_ENV=1` explicitly opt into the heavier backend-local diagnostic env path. `backend/scripts/run-dev.sh`, `backend/test/dev-start-script.test.js`, and `README.md` now lock down and document that reversed default.
- [x] The shared footer composer and active-overlay composer now keep local draft edits authoritative until the server echoes the same draft state back. `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now treats local mode, pin set, shared draft, and pinned draft mutations as pending local authority, so stale REST/WS echoes cannot overwrite newer in-flight typing in the footer or overlay composer paths. `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js` now locks down the stale shared-draft echo case for the shared footer path.
- [x] The active-terminal overlay composer no longer drops textarea focus on every keystroke. `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js` now keeps stable overlay hosts mounted across no-op rerenders instead of clearing and re-appending the shared overlay shell or pinned overlay root on every render pass, and `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js` now locks down that stable-mount contract for the shared overlay path.
- [x] Local development now has an explicit lightweight backend path so the default interactive runtime can avoid accidentally inheriting heavy backend-local analysis settings. Root `package.json` now exposes `npm run dev:light`, backend `package.json` now exposes `npm --prefix backend run dev:light`, and `backend/scripts/run-dev.sh` now honors `PTYDECK_BACKEND_SKIP_LOCAL_ENV=1` to skip auto-loading `local-config/ptydeck/backend.env.local`.
- [x] `backend/test/dev-start-script.test.js` now locks down both sides of that contract: the backend dev start script still auto-loads a provided local env file by default, and it now also fail-closed verifies that the same file is ignored when the lightweight skip flag is set. `README.md` now documents that debug logging, session-stream analysis capture, and messaging env in `local-config/ptydeck/backend.env.local` are diagnostic settings that can materially increase backend write pressure on busy multi-session local runs, and that `dev:light` is the normal low-overhead local path.
- [x] `CMP-402`, `CMP-403`, and `CMP-404` are now complete, fully closing `v0.4.0-H183` on `main`. The frontend now supports server-persisted operator-client composer placement modes through `frontend/src/public/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.js`, including a global `shared-footer` versus `active-overlay` mode switch, active-terminal overlay migration without terminal resize, per-terminal pin and unpin controls, concurrent pinned overlays, isolated pinned drafts, and shared-draft fallback only for non-pinned active terminals.
- [x] The control-pane shell and session-card surface were extended to support the new placement contract without widening the existing send semantics. `frontend/src/public/index.html`, `frontend/src/public/styles.css`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-dom-refs.js`, `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-factory-controller.js`, `frontend/src/public/ui/session-grid-controller.js`, and `frontend/src/public/ui/session-terminal-runtime-controller.js` now expose the mode selector, overlay host, and per-session pin affordance, while `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-composition-controller.js`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-access-control-composition.js`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-initialization-access-composition.js`, `frontend/src/public/app-command-ui-facade-controller.js`, `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-recovery-composition.js`, and `frontend/src/public/runtime-event-controller.js` now wire the server-side placement state through initial load, runtime snapshots, and targeted update events.
- [x] Focused regression coverage for the composer-placement feature now lives in `frontend/test/operator-composer-placement-runtime-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/api-client.test.js`, `frontend/test/runtime-event-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/template-contract.test.js`, `frontend/test/styles-contract.test.js`, and `frontend/test/app-architecture-closeout.test.js`, covering the REST contract, runtime snapshot/update application, shared overlay migration, pin lifecycle, draft isolation, and the required UI markup/CSS contract.
- [x] Session creation no longer risks turning a successful `POST /api/v1/sessions` into a late `404` when aggressive idle/max-lifetime guardrails sweep the new session before the follow-up session update broadcast. `backend/src/runtime-session-dispatch.js` now passes the freshly shaped API payload into `broadcastSessionUpdated(...)`, and `backend/src/runtime.js` now uses that fallback payload when the just-created session has already been removed by a guardrail race.
- [x] Closeout validation for `CMP-402` through `CMP-404` plus the session-create race fix passed with `npm run docs:generate`, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run lint`, `npm run test`, `npm run test:coverage:check`, and `git diff --check`. Final validated repo totals on the pushed tree are root tooling `92.84%` line / `77.05%` branch / `94.83%` funcs, backend `97.40%` line / `90.97%` branch / `87.77%` funcs, and frontend `97.21%` line / `90.85%` branch / `81.87%` funcs.

## 2026-05-12

- [x] `QLT-357` through `QLT-362` are now complete, fully closing `v0.4.0-H182` on `main`. The backend runtime now delegates retained startup/session-dispatch assembly into `backend/src/runtime-session-dispatch-assembly.js`, the backend session manager now delegates retained launch/reconnect/app-identity facade wiring into `backend/src/session-manager-runtime-facade.js`, the frontend runtime composition now delegates the retained startup/reclaim/operator helper bridge into `frontend/src/public/app-runtime-startup-helper-composition.js`, and shared layout/workspace selection shaping now lives in `frontend/src/public/layout-workspace-selection-state.js`.
- [x] Direct deterministic seam regressions for the `H182` extractions now live in `backend/test/runtime-session-dispatch-assembly.test.js`, `backend/test/session-manager-runtime-facade.test.js`, `frontend/test/app-runtime-startup-helper-composition.test.js`, and `frontend/test/layout-workspace-selection-state.test.js`, while retained runtime/operator interaction coverage was tightened in `backend/test/runtime-library-normalization.test.js`, `backend/test/runtime-session-messaging-authority.test.js`, `frontend/test/app-architecture-closeout.test.js`, `frontend/test/app-runtime-startup-helper-assembly.test.js`, `frontend/test/layout-workspace-runtime-state.test.js`, and `frontend/test/session-terminal-runtime-controller.test.js`.

## 2026-05-05

- [x] Fixed a multiline shell-send transport bug where text pasted into the command composer and sent through the normal `Send` path could reach shell-family sessions with the wrong internal line-separator semantics. `frontend/src/public/terminal-stream.js` now supports a session-aware multiline transport mode so shell-targeted sends can normalize internal separators to the configured terminator instead of always forcing raw `LF` inside the payload, while coding-agent and unknown sessions keep the previous `LF`-preserving behavior.
- [x] `frontend/src/public/command-composer-runtime-controller.js`, `frontend/src/public/command-executor-custom-handlers.js`, and `frontend/src/public/session-quick-send-runtime-controller.js` now route multiline shell-targeted sends through that configured-separator transport mode, covering the normal composer `Send` flow, custom command dispatch, and quick-send favorites without widening the shipped slash-command or quick-send contract.
- [x] Direct regression coverage for the multiline shell-send fix now lives in `frontend/test/terminal-stream.test.js`, `frontend/test/command-composer-runtime-controller.test.js`, `frontend/test/command-executor-custom-handlers.test.js`, and `frontend/test/session-quick-send-runtime-controller.test.js`, proving that shell-family multiline sends opt into configured internal separators while existing non-shell `LF` behavior remains intact.

- [x] One-shot `/ssh ...` launches now fail into the existing SSH trust workflow deterministically instead of forcing operators to rebuild the attempted target manually in the `Connections` UI. When no trusted host key exists yet, the attempted SSH launch now seeds the connection-profile draft with the same target/launch payload, auto-probes host keys for that target, and stops with the existing trust guidance so the operator can trust the matching key and rerun the same `/ssh ...` command.

- [x] The persistent restart/Codex analysis record now captures the stronger live conclusion from that experiment: the current `ptydeck` tails of `60` and `120` entries still classify as `restart_remount`, while tails of `300`, `600`, and `1200` entries decay only into `overlay_churn` rather than a `stable_section`; all of those tails still run at roughly `30` entries/sec, with dominant blank plus overlay-fragment ratios, no prompt/signal structure, and no strict message candidates surviving after cleanup, so `docs/Restart Streaming Analysis.md`, `docs/README.md`, and `CODEX_CONTEXT.md` now record that window-state-first gating is the necessary next step before any later clean-delivery candidate logic.

- [x] `v0.4.0-H96` is now completed on `main`, extending the Codex offline-analysis path with an explicit restart/remount stability experiment instead of returning to product-side message tuning.
- [x] The candidate-experiment helper `scripts/experiment-codex-candidates.mjs` now distinguishes between section-level candidate extraction and capture-window stability, adding a `stableStrict` mode that only keeps direct-after-separator `info` blocks when the live capture tail itself contains enough substantial Codex data instead of mostly blank, overlay, or background-ribbon churn.
- [x] The persistent analysis docs now record the key outcome of that experiment: for the current live `ptydeck` session, tails of `60`, `120`, `300`, `600`, and `1200` entries all fail the stability gate and therefore produce zero `stableStrict` candidates, while the visual dump files still demonstrate that section semantics alone are not selective enough for final delivery decisions; `docs/Restart Streaming Analysis.md` and `CODEX_CONTEXT.md` now capture that split explicitly.

- [x] The repository now includes `scripts/experiment-codex-candidates.mjs`, which applies a deliberately conservative block-first rule to visual Codex dumps and persisted JSONL raw-stream capture: filter to substantial Codex entries, drop blank/overlay/background-terminal ribbon churn, parse major separators plus `•` blocks, type those blocks (`info`, `ran`, `explored`, `waited`, `context_compacted`, `updated_plan`), and report only direct-after-separator `info` candidates in loose and strict modes.

- [x] Fixed the remaining deck-specific direct-terminal-input regression in `frontend/src/public/ui/session-grid-controller.js`: the grid reorder check now compares against the full globally ordered session list instead of only the active deck subset, so normal renders on non-first decks no longer re-append already correct terminal cards and break xterm typing after one or two characters.
- [x] Root cause was a mismatch between the deck-local reorder input and the real workspace DOM contents: the grid contains terminal cards for all sessions across all decks, but the earlier reorder guard only compared the current deck subset, which made the browser repeatedly move cards on certain decks even when nothing had actually changed for that deck's visible order.
- [x] Added focused regression coverage in `frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js` proving that when the global DOM order already matches all sessions, rendering a different active deck does not re-append that deck's cards.

- [x] Fixed a remaining frontend direct-terminal-input regression in `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-render-controller.js`: when a normal session-card render interrupts a currently focused xterm mount, the controller now explicitly restores xterm focus for the active visible session instead of leaving the browser in a post-render blur state.
- [x] The additional regression guard in `frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js` now proves that activity-driven card renders preserve direct terminal typing by refocusing the terminal when the pre-render active element was inside the session mount.

- [x] Fixed a frontend terminal-input stall caused by hidden settings-control churn during normal session renders: `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-render-controller.js` no longer re-syncs startup, input-safety, and theme settings controls for closed dialogs on every runtime render, and `frontend/src/public/ui/session-card-interactions-controller.js` now refreshes those controls only when the settings dialog is explicitly opened.
- [x] Root cause was repeated rebuilding of hidden per-session settings controls, especially the theme preset `<select>` with hundreds of options, during ordinary activity-driven workspace renders; that created enough DOM churn to stall the browser main thread and make direct xterm keyboard input unreliable even though middle-click paste and the backend `/input` endpoint still behaved correctly.
- [x] Added focused regression coverage in `frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js` and `frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js` proving that hidden dialogs no longer resync heavyweight controls during ordinary card updates while settings dialogs still receive a fresh control sync when explicitly opened.
- [x] Validation for the terminal-input/render-churn hotfix passed with targeted frontend regressions (`node --test frontend/test/session-card-render-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-card-interactions-controller.test.js frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js frontend/test/app.test.js`) plus the full local quality gate (`npm run lint`, `npm run test`, `npm run test:coverage:check`), with no leftover background validation processes after completion.

- [x] Fixed a frontend direct-terminal-input regression introduced by the quick-ID ordering hotfix: `frontend/src/public/ui/session-grid-controller.js` no longer re-appends already ordered terminal cards on every workspace render, so xterm focus stays stable while typing directly into the terminal surface.
- [x] Root cause was unconditional `gridEl.appendChild(entry.element)` calls for every existing terminal card during each render pass; that kept the H24 quick-ID ordering visible, but it also moved focused DOM nodes repeatedly and made direct terminal typing unreliable even though the input box path still worked.
- [x] Existing session cards are now reordered only when the desired quick-ID order actually differs from the current DOM order, preserving visible `/swap`-driven reordering while avoiding unnecessary DOM moves during normal activity/render cycles.
- [x] Added focused regression coverage in `frontend/test/session-grid-controller.test.js` to prove both behaviors: cards still reorder when quick-ID order changes, and cards are not re-appended when the DOM order already matches the desired order.

- [x] Root cause was a DOM-clearing helper that only removed children when `element.children` was a plain array; real browser `SELECT` elements expose collection-like children instead, so stale layout-profile entries remained visible after reloads, saves, renames, and deletes even though the backend profile state itself was not duplicated.

## 2026-03-26

- [x] `DRV-001` is now completed: backend shell-specific CWD tracking no longer lives as a bash-only inline special case inside `SessionManager`; it now flows through a dedicated shell-adapter abstraction with an explicit capability matrix and deterministic fallback behavior for unsupported shells.
- [x] Added `backend/src/shell-adapter.js`, which now defines the per-shell CWD-tracking capability matrix (`bash`, `zsh`, `fish`, `posix_sh`, `unknown`), keeps bash on the `PROMPT_COMMAND` marker path, and makes unsupported shells explicitly retain the last known CWD without prompt-hook mutation.
- [x] `backend/src/session-manager.js` now delegates spawn-env preparation and output cleanup to the resolved shell adapter, so bash marker injection/consumption stays supported while unsupported shells keep their spawn environment/output unchanged and fall back deterministically to the persisted/start CWD.
- [x] Added focused regression coverage in `backend/test/shell-adapter.test.js` and extended `backend/test/session-manager.test.js` to prove the explicit capability matrix, bash marker behavior, split-marker handling, and unsupported-shell fallback semantics.
- [x] `backend/package.json` syntax-check gates now include `src/shell-adapter.js`, and `README.md` now documents the current shell capability matrix for working-directory tracking instead of implying a generic shell-wide `PROMPT_COMMAND` solution.

## 2026-03-23

- [x] Frontend hotfix for hidden-deck rendering drift: terminal cards now mark pending viewport sync while hidden and run forced resize/refresh passes when shown again.
- [x] Deck-switch visibility path now preserves and reapplies follow-to-bottom behavior (`scrollToBottom`) for sessions that were at the buffer end before being hidden.
- [x] Background-output deck switch regression hardening: hidden sessions with incoming output now recover full scrollability/content reachability after returning to the deck.
- [x] Frontend regression coverage now simulates hidden-deck background output and verifies viewport recovery (`refresh` + `scrollToBottom`) after switching back.
- [x] Frontend deck isolation hardening: resize/mount geometry is now derived from the owning session deck instead of the globally active deck, preventing hidden deck sessions from inheriting foreign `cols/rows` on deck switch.
- [x] Deferred resize passes triggered by deck switching are now scoped to the active deck, eliminating delayed cross-deck geometry bleed.
- [x] Frontend regression coverage now asserts that a hidden session in another deck retains its own configured `cols/rows` after switching to a differently sized deck.
- [x] Frontend deck-switch hotfix: geometry/resize updates are now scoped to the active deck during deck changes, preventing cross-deck resize side effects when decks use different `cols`/`rows`.
- [x] Deck switching now applies visibility first and then triggers deck-scoped forced resize passes, reducing transient render artifacts while switching between decks.
- [x] Frontend regression timing updated to keep resize assertions deterministic under deck-scoped resize scheduling.
- [x] Hotfix: `/move` no longer fails with `JSON.parse: unexpected end of data` when backend returns `204 No Content` for `POST /api/v1/decks/{deckId}/sessions/{sessionId}:move`.
- [x] Frontend API client now treats deck move as `204` operation and then reloads moved session via `GET /api/v1/sessions/{sessionId}` to keep FE state deterministic.
- [x] Regression coverage updated in frontend API and app integration tests for the move flow with follow-up session read.
- [x] Deck navigation UI moved from workspace top header into the left sidebar to preserve vertical terminal space.
- [x] Sidebar deck section now uses a vertical deck list with full-width deck action buttons (`New/Rename/Delete`) and keeps active-deck switching in the sidebar.
- [x] Backend WS payload hardening: `snapshot.sessions` and `session.created.session` are now emitted in API-session shape (including `deckId`) to prevent FE reload from reverting deck assignment to default.
- [x] Added WS regression assertions for `deckId` presence in `session.created` and reconnect snapshot session payloads.
- [x] `QLT-093` completed: frontend regression matrix now covers deck migration/CRUD conflict paths, active-deck isolation fallback behavior, slash deck routing, selector dedupe, and per-deck settings persistence with deterministic assertions.
- [x] Frontend deck regression tests were stabilized against selector/terminator edge cases by switching brittle last-payload assertions to deterministic target/count assertions and explicit selector scopes where needed.

- [x] Frontend filter UX hardening: `/filter` state is now persisted across hard reload (`Ctrl+F5`) and restored on startup from local storage.
- [x] Frontend filter behavior hardening: when active terminal is outside the current display filter, active focus now switches deterministically to the first visible filtered terminal.

- [x] Frontend terminal viewport hotfix: mount-height calibration now uses runtime xterm cell-height feedback to prevent bottom-row clipping in TUI apps (for example Codex-like prompts).
- [x] Backend persistence hotfix: mutating REST operations now persist synchronously (`session.create`, `session.update`, `session.delete`, `session.restart`, custom-command upsert/delete) so newly created sessions are on disk before API success responses.
- [x] Added backend regression coverage for immediate persistence durability after session create response.
- [x] Backend restore hardening: restore now applies multi-step shell/cwd fallbacks (`saved shell/cwd`, fallback shell + saved cwd, saved shell + home, fallback shell + home) instead of dropping sessions on first spawn failure.
- [x] Added backend regression coverage for restore fallback behavior (`invalid startCwd` and `invalid shell` persisted states).
- [x] Backend durability guard: unrestorable persisted sessions are now retained in runtime persistence snapshots (`unrestoredSessions`) so they are never silently removed from persisted state during subsequent save cycles.

## Maintenance Rules

- Move tasks to `CHANGELOG.md` only after implementation and verification.
- Keep entries factual and traceable to commits.
- Keep chronological order by date.
